### Task 4：图表区域识别、图表文字无损剥离、图片裁剪入库与数字召回诊断

> **2026-10-13 修订（用户裁决）：** 图表（柱状图、折线图、饼图等）里的信息**不提取**。T4 只做四件事：识别图表区域 → 把区域内的文字碎片从正文流中**无损剥离**（与页眉页脚剥离同一机制：记入 `stripped_text`，可审计）→ 把区域裁成 PNG 存入对象存储 → 产出一条图表元数据（`FigureRegion` / `FigureRef`）。该页**不再因图表碎片降级**；只有"有碎片却没检出区域"（检测失败）的页才降级（`chart_unresolved`）。**表格不在此列**：表格内容仍需逐格解析入库（`table` 块），图表检测器**永远不得吞掉表格**——表格区域优先（见下"表格优先"），表格页照旧去 L1 / L3。
>
> **为什么不需要任何闭合豁免：** 图表碎片（刻度、数据标签、图例）被剥离后不再进入正文，排版器根本看不到它们；`order_violation(markdown, original_text, stripped_text)` 的定义是"原始文字层的数字 − 输出数字 = 被剥离文字的数字"，被剥的图表数字正好落在等式右边。v1 唯一的误报（`( 4 %` 与 `)` 被分到两处，输出读成 `4%`）在新设计下是"两段碎片都被剥离、正文里没有这个数"，闭合照常通过；而**闭合校验本身一个字都不改**（保留"`(4%)` 读成 `4%` 必须被拦下"的测试）。
>
> **检索层不解读图片：** 图片只存、只带元数据，**不送任何视觉模型、不做 OCR**——与 `parse/textin.py` 里 `"apply_image_analysis": 0`（"会把图片送去大模型解读，越过计算/生成分离的边界"）同一理由；TextIn 请求参数（`get_image: none`、`apply_image_analysis: 0`）不改。图表里的数据因此**不可检索**（已知局限）。

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/chart.py`（图表区域检测与文字剥离，纯函数）、`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/figures.py`（`FigureRegion` / `FigureRef` / `FigureSkip`、按区域渲染裁剪 PNG 并内容寻址写入对象存储；放在 `parse/` 而非 `parse/local/`，因为 T9 的 TextIn 文档回填也用它）、`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/recall_diag.py`
- Modify: `packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/layout.py`（新增 `Graphic`，`PageLayout` 末尾加字段 `graphics: tuple[Graphic, ...] = ()`，`extract_pages` 填入 `page.images / rects / lines / curves` 的外接框）、`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/decor.py`（加公开名 `body_text = _body_text`，不改行为）、`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/samples.py`（夹具读写带上 `graphics`，缺省为空，旧夹具照样能读）、`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/pipeline.py`（公开 `self_check`）、`scripts/l0v2.py`（新增 `diag-v1`；`multicol-eval` 改为"先剥图表再判多栏"并加 `--charts on|off`；`fixtures` 重新生成带 `graphics` 的夹具）、`packages/ragdemo/pyproject.toml`（`dependencies` 加 `"pypdfium2>=5.13"`、`"pillow>=12.3"`——两者已因 pdfplumber 在 `uv.lock` 里，业务代码现在直接 import 它们，所以写成直接依赖；`uv lock` 后锁文件不得出现新包）
- Test: `tests/parse/local/test_chart.py`、`tests/parse/test_figures.py`、`tests/parse/local/test_recall_diag.py`
- Regenerate（入库）: `tests/fixtures/layouts/*.json`（8 个夹具加 `graphics` 键；`words` 必须与 HEAD 逐项相同，见 Step 5）
- Create: `docs/10.12-plan/验收记录/L0v2-调参记录.md` 追加"§1 数字召回诊断"与"§2 T4 后多栏复测"（文件已由 T2 创建）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Word`、`PageLayout`、`extract_pages`（layout）；`strip_decor`、`TOP_BAND`、`BOTTOM_BAND`、`body_text`（decor）；`cluster_rows`、`find_table_regions`、`split_segments`（tables）；`body_font_size`、`typeset_document`（typeset）；`page_columns`（T3）；`numbers_in`、`fold_text`（number_closure）；`_self_check`、`order_violation`（pipeline）；`plain_text_from_markdown`、`number_recall_precision`（compare）；`BlobStore`、`LocalBlobStore`（`ragdemo_core.blob`）；`pypdfium2.PdfDocument`、`PIL.Image`；v1 产物 `data/experiments/parse-compare-det-docs-2026-10-12.tsv` 与 `data/experiments/det-md/<doc_id>/pNNN.<status>.md`。
- Produces（T5 / T7 / T9 / T10 使用）：
  - layout：`GraphicKind = Literal["image", "rect", "line", "curve"]`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) Graphic(kind: GraphicKind, x0: float, x1: float, top: float, bottom: float, filled: bool = False, points: int = 0)`（属性 `width`、`height`）；`PageLayout.graphics: tuple[Graphic, ...] = ()`
  - figures（`ragdemo.parse.figures`）：`BBox = tuple[float, float, float, float]`（`(x0, top, x1, bottom)`，单位 pt，**原点 = 页面左上角，x 向右、y 向下**，即 pdfplumber 坐标）；`Detection = Literal["embedded_image", "vector"]`；`FigureSource = Literal["local", "textin"]`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) FigureRegion(page: int, bbox: BBox, detection: Detection, caption: str = "", source_note: str = "", stripped_words: int = 0)`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) FigureRef(doc_sha256: str, page: int, bbox_pt: BBox, bbox_norm: BBox, image_key: str, image_sha256: str, width_px: int, height_px: int, dpi: int, png_bytes: int, caption: str, source_note: str, source: FigureSource, detection: Detection, detector_version: str, stripped_words: int)`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) FigureSkip(page: int, bbox: BBox, reason: str)`；常量 `FIGURE_DPI = 120`、`FIGURE_FALLBACK_DPI = 96`、`FIGURE_MAX_EDGE_PX = 1600`、`FIGURE_MAX_BYTES = 1_000_000`、`FIGURE_PAD_PT = 2.0`、`FIGURE_KEY_PREFIX = "parse/figures"`、`PAGE_SIZE_TOL_PT = 0.5`；`figure_key(doc_sha256: str, png: bytes) -> tuple[str, str]`；`crop_figures(pdf_bytes: bytes, regions: Sequence[FigureRegion], *, doc_sha256: str, blob: BlobStore, source: FigureSource, detector_version: str, page_sizes: Mapping[int, tuple[float, float]]) -> tuple[list[FigureRef], list[FigureSkip]]`；`figure_to_dict(ref: FigureRef) -> dict[str, object]`；`figure_from_dict(d: Mapping[str, Any]) -> FigureRef`
  - chart：`CHART_DETECTOR_VERSION: Final = "chart-v1"`；检测常量（见下与"调参空间登记"）；`REASON_FIGURE: Final = "figure"`；`REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED: Final = "图表区域内的非水平文字含数字"`；`REASON_CHART_UNRESOLVED: Final = "图表碎片残留（未检出图表区域）"`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) ChartStrip(layout: PageLayout, figures: tuple[FigureRegion, ...] = (), items: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (), discarded: tuple[str, ...] = (), unsafe_reason: str | None = None)`（属性 `stripped_text: str`、`stripped_words: int`）；`embedded_image_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], width: float, height: float) -> list[BBox]`；`grid_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]], height: float) -> list[BBox]`；`plot_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], width: float, height: float, grids: Sequence[BBox]) -> list[BBox]`；`is_caption_text(text: str) -> bool`；`is_source_note(text: str) -> bool`；`is_fragment_row(row: Sequence[Word]) -> bool`；`residual_fragment_rows(rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]]) -> int`；`strip_charts(layout: PageLayout, body_size: float) -> ChartStrip`
  - recall_diag：`MissingNumbers(reference_total, hit, decor_stripped, tokenization, not_in_text_layer, unexplained, examples, figure_stripped: int = 0)`（新字段**追加在末尾**，位置参数构造不受影响；`plus` 同步累加）；`classify_missing_numbers(reference: str, candidate: str, *, layer_text: str, stripped_text: str, figure_text: str = "") -> MissingNumbers`
  - pipeline：`self_check = _self_check`（签名 `(markdown: str, source: str, tables: Sequence[str], original_text: str, stripped_text: str) -> tuple[list[str], str]`，顺序检查以未剥离的原始 extract_text 为基准）
  - 脚本：`diag-v1`（输出 `data/experiments/l0v2/diag-v1-recall.tsv`）；`multicol-eval --set tune --charts on|off`

**设计要点（实现子代理必须照此实现，偏离须报 BLOCKED）：**

1. **两类来源，检测只看几何。** (a) 嵌入图片：`page.images` 的外接框，过滤装饰——面积 < 页面 `IMG_MIN_AREA_SHARE` 或短边 < `IMG_MIN_SIDE_PT`（图标）、面积 > `IMG_MAX_AREA_SHARE`（整页底图 / 水印）、完全落在页眉 / 页脚带（`decor.TOP_BAND` / `BOTTOM_BAND`）且面积 < `BAND_MAX_AREA_SHARE`（logo）、长边 ≤ `SQUARE_MAX_SIDE_PT` 且宽高比在 `SQUARE_ASPECT` 内（二维码 / 方形 logo）。(b) 矢量图：`rects / lines / curves` 去掉页眉页脚带内的元素与"表格网格"元素后，按外扩 `VECTOR_JOIN_PT` 的外接框相交做连通聚类；一个簇是图表当且仅当元素数 ≥ `VECTOR_MIN_ELEMENTS`、面积 ≥ 页面 `VECTOR_MIN_AREA_SHARE`、且有**绘图标记**：填充矩形（短边 > `RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT`、且面积 < 簇外接框 `BACKGROUND_AREA_SHARE`，即排除底色面板）≥ `MIN_BARS`，或点数 ≥ `CURVE_MIN_POINTS` 的曲线 / 折线（非底色）≥ 1，或斜线（宽高都 > `RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT`）≥ `MIN_DIAGONALS`。
2. **表格 vs 图表（表格优先）。** "表格网格" = 只由细线（`RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT` 以内的线 / 细矩形）组成的簇里，横线（长度 ≥ 簇宽 `GRID_SPAN_SHARE`）有 ≥ `GRID_MIN_H_RULES` 条不同 y、竖线（长度 ≥ 簇高 `GRID_SPAN_SHARE`）有 ≥ `GRID_MIN_V_RULES` 条不同 x，**框内没有任何绘图标记**，且框内有 ≥ 2 行"按 `FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP` 切成 ≥ 2 段"的文字行（对齐的文字列）。图表的坐标轴 + 水平网格线最多一条贯通竖线，且框内有柱 / 线标记——两条都不满足网格定义。**受保护的词**（永不剥离）= 表格网格框内的词 ∪ `find_table_regions` 区域里**落在所有候选绘图框之外**的词（绘图框内部的是图表自己的数据标签，只是被行聚类与旁边的表格行并到了同一行）∪ 图注 / 资料来源行的词。外扩标签带里的受保护词一律不剥（图表正下方紧跟的表格行、与表格并排的刻度都留在正文，页面按原规则判 `table`）。候选图表区域的**绘图框内部**只要有一个受保护的表格词，整块区域**放弃**（一个字都不剥、不出图，记入 `discarded`），该页按原规则判型（多半是 `table` → L1 / L3）。
3. **剥离范围 = 绘图框内部的词 + 绘图框外 `LABEL_MARGIN_EM` 个正文字号内的"标签段"。** 标签段 = 一行按 `FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP` 切出的段，段内每个词的中心都在外扩框内、段的紧凑长度 ≤ `LABEL_MAX_CHARS`，且不是受保护的词——所以紧邻的双栏正文（一个长段延伸到外扩框之外）、表格行（受保护）都不会被剥。绘图框内部若有一行不是标签行（存在紧凑长度 > `LABEL_MAX_CHARS` 的段：评级框、侧栏句子），判为"不是图表"放弃。区域最终外接框 = 绘图框 ∪ 被剥标签词的框（裁出的图片含坐标轴标签）。与已接受区域面积重叠 ≥ 80% 的候选去重。
4. **图注不剥离。** 匹配 `^(?:图表?|Figure|Fig\.|Chart)\s*[0-9０-９一二三四五六七八九十]+` 的行（`图 3：…`）与匹配 `^(?:资料来源|数据来源|来源)\s*[:：]` 的行是**正文行**，留在正文流里；与区域上 / 下边缘纵向距离 ≤ `CAPTION_MAX_GAP_EM` 个字号、且与区域横向有重叠的最近一行，原文写入 `FigureRegion.caption` / `source_note`（取自文字层，逐字，可溯源）；没有就空串。
5. **方向安全沿用既有规则。** 水平与否只看 `Word.upright`（由字符文本矩阵判定，`layout.char_is_horizontal`），**不得重新引入 pdfminer 的 `upright` 标志**。区域内（含外扩标签带）的非水平词（竖排坐标轴标题）一并剥离；其中任何一个含数字 → `ChartStrip.unsafe_reason = REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED`，由 T5 整页判 `multicolumn`（与"被剥离的非水平文字含数字整页退回"同一原则）。
6. **无损拆分。** 被剥的词按行聚成 `("figure", 行文字)` 记入 `ChartStrip.items`（行内词用一个空格连接，与 `decor._row_text` 相同）；`ChartStrip.layout` = 原版面去掉这些词，`text = decor.body_text(剩余词)`。不变量：剩余词的内容字符多重集合 + 被剥文字的内容字符多重集合 == 原版面词的内容字符多重集合（测试与 T5 的 G2 审计都检查）。
7. **版面分流次序：先剥图表，再判多栏。** 图表里零散的标签会造成"纵向空白带"误报（评审已指出），所以 T5 的 `classify_page` 与本任务改过的 `multicol-eval` 都对 `strip_charts(...).layout` 做多栏判定；T3 的多栏调参结果因此要在本任务 Step 7 **复测**。
8. **检出图表的页照样走 L0。** 图表区域变成元数据（`FigureRegion`，GO 后成为 `figure` 块），其余正文照常排版、照常自检；检测失败（有 ≥ `RESIDUAL_MIN_FRAGMENT_ROWS` 行碎片却没被任何区域覆盖）或方向不安全的页按既有规则降级。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
# tests/parse/local/test_chart.py
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from reportlab.lib.utils import ImageReader
from reportlab.pdfbase import pdfmetrics
from reportlab.pdfbase.cidfonts import UnicodeCIDFont
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas

from ragdemo.parse.local.chart import (
    REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED,
    ChartStrip,
    is_caption_text,
    is_fragment_row,
    residual_fragment_rows,
    strip_charts,
)
from ragdemo.parse.local.columns import page_columns
from ragdemo.parse.local.decor import strip_decor
from ragdemo.parse.local.layout import PageLayout, Word, extract_pages
from ragdemo.parse.local.pipeline import self_check
from ragdemo.parse.local.samples import layout_from_fixture
from ragdemo.parse.local.tables import cluster_rows, find_table_regions
from ragdemo.parse.local.typeset import body_font_size, typeset_document
from tests.parse.local.test_typeset import LEAD, body_lines, line

PW, PH = 595.0, 842.0
FONT = "STSong-Light"
SENTENCE = "这是一段用于测试的正文文字句子足够长不会被当作图表标签"
TABLE = [("营业收入", "91.2", "60.1"), ("营业成本", "40.5", "30.2"), ("毛利润", "50.7", "29.9"), ("净利润", "20.3", "11.8")]
RAW_RESEARCH = Path("docs/rawdata/688041-hygon/research")


def text(c: canvas.Canvas, s: str, x: float, top: float, size: float = 10.0) -> None:
    """top = 基线到页顶的距离（pt）。"""
    c.setFont(FONT, size)
    c.drawString(x, PH - top, s)


def body(c: canvas.Canvas, top: float, n: int, x: float = 60.0, s: str = SENTENCE) -> None:
    for i in range(n):
        text(c, s, x, top + i * 15)


def bar_chart(c: canvas.Canvas, x0: float, top: float, w: float = 200.0, h: float = 140.0) -> None:
    base = PH - (top + h)
    c.setLineWidth(0.5)
    c.line(x0, base, x0 + w, base)
    c.line(x0, base, x0, base + h)
    for k in (1, 2):
        c.line(x0, base + k * h / 2, x0 + w, base + k * h / 2)
    for i, v in enumerate((40, 25, 34, 12)):
        c.setFillColorRGB(0.2, 0.4, 0.8)
        c.rect(x0 + 15 + i * 45, base, 26, v * 3, stroke=0, fill=1)
        c.setFillColorRGB(0, 0, 0)
        text(c, f"{v}%", x0 + 17 + i * 45, top + h - v * 3 - 3, size=7)
        text(c, str(2021 + i), x0 + 15 + i * 45, top + h + 10, size=7)
    for k, t in enumerate(("40", "20", "0")):
        text(c, t, x0 - 14, top + k * h / 2 + 3, size=7)


def line_chart(c: canvas.Canvas, x0: float, top: float, w: float = 200.0, h: float = 140.0) -> None:
    base = PH - (top + h)
    c.setLineWidth(0.5)
    c.line(x0, base, x0 + w, base)
    c.line(x0, base, x0, base + h)
    c.line(x0, base + h / 2, x0 + w, base + h / 2)
    p = c.beginPath()
    p.moveTo(x0 + 10, base + 20)
    for i, y in enumerate((60, 45, 100, 120), start=1):
        p.lineTo(x0 + 10 + i * 45, base + y)
    c.drawPath(p, stroke=1, fill=0)
    for i in range(5):
        text(c, str(2020 + i), x0 + 2 + i * 45, top + h + 10, size=7)


def plain_table(c: canvas.Canvas, top: float, xs: tuple[float, float, float] = (60.0, 200.0, 340.0)) -> None:
    for i, row in enumerate(TABLE):
        for x, cell in zip(xs, row, strict=True):
            text(c, cell, x, top + i * 15)


def ruled_table(c: canvas.Canvas, x0: float, top: float) -> None:
    widths, row_h = (90.0, 70.0, 70.0), 16.0
    right = x0 + sum(widths)
    c.setLineWidth(0.5)
    for i in range(len(TABLE) + 1):
        y = PH - (top + i * row_h)
        c.line(x0, y, right, y)
    x = x0
    for wd in (0.0, *widths):
        x += wd
        c.line(x, PH - top, x, PH - (top + len(TABLE) * row_h))
    for i, row in enumerate(TABLE):
        x = x0
        for wd, cell in zip(widths, row, strict=True):
            text(c, cell, x + 4, top + i * row_h + 12)
            x += wd


def pdf_page(draw: Callable[[canvas.Canvas], None]) -> bytes:
    pdfmetrics.registerFont(UnicodeCIDFont(FONT))
    buf = BytesIO()
    c = canvas.Canvas(buf, pagesize=(PW, PH))
    draw(c)
    c.showPage()
    c.save()
    return buf.getvalue()


def strip_first(pdf: bytes) -> tuple[PageLayout, ChartStrip]:
    (lay,) = extract_pages(pdf)
    return lay, strip_charts(lay, body_font_size([lay]))


def alnum(texts: Iterable[str]) -> Counter[str]:
    return Counter(ch for t in texts for ch in t if ch.isalnum())


def assert_lossless(lay: PageLayout, cs: ChartStrip) -> None:
    assert alnum(w.text for w in lay.words) == alnum(w.text for w in cs.layout.words) + alnum([cs.stripped_text])


def bar_page(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
    body(c, 60, 3)
    text(c, "图1：营业收入与增速", 60, 106, size=9)
    bar_chart(c, 80, 120)
    text(c, "资料来源：公司公告，测试整理", 60, 296, size=8)
    body(c, 330, 3)


def test_extract_pages_reports_images_rects_lines_and_curves_as_graphics() -> None:
    def draw(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
        bar_chart(c, 80, 120)
        line_chart(c, 320, 120)
        c.drawImage(ImageReader(Image.new("RGB", (40, 30), (200, 220, 240))), 60, 400, 120, 90)

    (lay,) = extract_pages(pdf_page(draw))
    kinds = Counter(g.kind for g in lay.graphics)
    assert kinds["image"] == 1 and kinds["rect"] >= 4 and kinds["line"] >= 6 and kinds["curve"] >= 1
    assert all(g.x0 <= g.x1 and g.top <= g.bottom for g in lay.graphics)


def test_a_vector_bar_chart_is_one_figure_its_labels_are_stripped_and_the_caption_stays() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(bar_page))
    (fig,) = cs.figures
    assert fig.detection == "vector" and fig.caption == "图1：营业收入与增速"
    assert fig.source_note == "资料来源：公司公告，测试整理"
    body_texts = {w.text for w in cs.layout.words}
    assert not body_texts & {"40%", "25%", "34%", "12%", "2021", "2024", "40", "20", "0"}
    assert any("图1" in t for t in body_texts) and any("资料来源" in t for t in body_texts)
    assert sum(1 for w in cs.layout.words if w.text == SENTENCE) == 6
    assert fig.stripped_words == cs.stripped_words == len(lay.words) - len(cs.layout.words)
    x0, top, x1, bottom = fig.bbox
    assert x0 <= 66 and top <= 122 and x1 >= 280 and bottom >= 262  # 绘图框 ∪ 标签
    assert_lossless(lay, cs)


def test_a_line_chart_drawn_as_a_polyline_is_a_figure() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 3), line_chart(c, 80, 140), body(c, 330, 3))))
    (fig,) = cs.figures
    assert fig.detection == "vector" and not {"2020", "2024"} & {w.text for w in cs.layout.words}
    assert_lossless(lay, cs)


def test_an_embedded_chart_image_is_a_figure_and_a_header_logo_and_a_qr_code_are_not() -> None:
    def draw(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
        img = ImageReader(Image.new("RGB", (400, 250), (200, 220, 240)))
        c.drawImage(ImageReader(Image.new("RGB", (40, 40), (0, 0, 0))), 40, PH - 50, 36, 36)  # 页眉 logo
        body(c, 90, 3)
        c.drawImage(img, 60, PH - 300, 260, 160)  # top=140 bottom=300
        c.drawImage(ImageReader(Image.new("RGB", (70, 70), (0, 0, 0))), 450, PH - 680, 70, 70)  # 二维码
        body(c, 330, 3)

    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(draw))
    (fig,) = cs.figures
    assert fig.detection == "embedded_image"
    assert fig.bbox == pytest.approx((60.0, 140.0, 320.0, 300.0), abs=1.0)
    assert cs.stripped_words == 0 and len(cs.layout.words) == len(lay.words)


def test_a_ruled_grid_with_cell_text_is_a_table_not_a_chart_and_nothing_is_stripped() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 3), ruled_table(c, 60, 140), body(c, 260, 3))))
    assert cs.figures == () and cs.items == ()
    assert len(cs.layout.words) == len(lay.words)


def _table_words(lay: PageLayout) -> list[Word]:
    cells = {cell for row in TABLE for cell in row}
    return [w for w in lay.words if w.text in cells]


def test_a_chart_directly_above_a_table_keeps_every_table_word_in_the_body() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 2), bar_chart(c, 80, 120), plain_table(c, 284))))
    assert len(cs.figures) == 1
    kept = {id(w) for w in cs.layout.words}
    assert len(_table_words(lay)) == 12 and all(id(w) in kept for w in _table_words(lay))
    assert not any(cell in cs.stripped_text for row in TABLE for cell in row)
    rows = cluster_rows([w for w in cs.layout.words if w.upright])
    assert any(sum(1 for r in reg for w in r if w.text in {"营业收入", "净利润"}) == 2 for reg in find_table_regions(rows))
    assert_lossless(lay, cs)


def test_a_chart_beside_a_table_keeps_every_table_word_in_the_body() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (bar_chart(c, 80, 120), plain_table(c, 160, xs=(330.0, 420.0, 500.0)))))
    assert len(cs.figures) == 1
    kept = {id(w) for w in cs.layout.words}
    assert all(id(w) in kept for w in _table_words(lay))
    assert_lossless(lay, cs)


def test_a_chart_next_to_two_column_body_text_strips_only_the_chart() -> None:
    col = "左栏正文文字句子足够长不会被当作标签"

    def draw(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
        body(c, 100, 10, x=320, s="右栏正文文字句子足够长不会被当作标签")
        body(c, 100, 3, x=60, s=col)
        bar_chart(c, 80, 160)

    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(draw))
    assert len(cs.figures) == 1
    assert sum(1 for w in cs.layout.words if w.text.startswith("右栏")) == 10
    assert sum(1 for w in cs.layout.words if w.text == col) == 3
    assert page_columns(cs.layout, body_font_size([lay])).multicolumn  # 双栏正文照样被拦
    assert_lossless(lay, cs)


def _rotated(c: canvas.Canvas, s: str, x: float, top: float) -> None:
    c.saveState()
    c.translate(x, PH - top)
    c.rotate(90)
    c.setFont(FONT, 7)
    c.drawString(0, 0, s)
    c.restoreState()


def test_a_rotated_axis_title_without_digits_is_stripped_and_one_with_digits_is_unsafe() -> None:
    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 3), bar_chart(c, 90, 140), _rotated(c, "单位亿元", 70, 240))))
    assert len(cs.figures) == 1 and cs.unsafe_reason is None
    assert all(w.upright for w in cs.layout.words) and "单位亿元" in cs.stripped_text.replace(" ", "")
    _, bad = strip_first(pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 3), bar_chart(c, 90, 140), _rotated(c, "2024亿元", 70, 240))))
    assert bad.unsafe_reason == REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED


def test_a_filled_sidebar_panel_with_sentences_is_not_a_chart() -> None:
    def draw(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
        c.setFillColorRGB(0.9, 0.9, 0.95)
        c.roundRect(380, PH - 500, 180, 380, 8, stroke=0, fill=1)
        c.setFillColorRGB(0.8, 0.1, 0.1)
        for i in range(3):
            c.rect(390 + i * 14, PH - 140, 10, 10, stroke=0, fill=1)
        c.setFillColorRGB(0, 0, 0)
        body(c, 170, 6, x=390, s="分析师执业证书编号与联系方式说明")
        body(c, 100, 12, x=60, s="正文")

    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(draw))
    assert cs.figures == () and cs.items == () and len(cs.layout.words) == len(lay.words)


def test_the_bracketed_negative_split_inside_a_chart_is_stripped_and_the_unchanged_closure_passes() -> None:
    def draw(c: canvas.Canvas) -> None:
        body(c, 80, 3, s="本期营业收入同比增长百分之十二点五正文")
        bar_chart(c, 80, 120)
        text(c, "(4%", 200, 200, size=7)
        text(c, ")", 216, 200, size=7)
        body(c, 330, 3, s="下文继续说明经营情况正文句子足够长")

    lay, cs = strip_first(pdf_page(draw))
    assert len(cs.figures) == 1 and "(4%" in cs.stripped_text and ")" in cs.stripped_text
    (res,) = typeset_document([cs.layout])
    assert res.status == "ok"
    _, reason = self_check(res.markdown, cs.layout.text, res.tables, lay.text, cs.stripped_text)
    assert reason == ""


def test_the_bracket_split_in_body_text_is_still_a_closure_failure_no_exemption() -> None:
    # v1 唯一误报的最小复现：原文 "(4%)"（括号即负数），排版后括号与数字分开 → 读成 "4%"。
    # 闭合校验不改、不豁免：没被图表区域剥走时，自检必须照样拦下。
    _, reason = self_check("4 %\n\n)", "( 4 %\n)", [], "( 4 %\n)", "")
    assert reason != ""


def test_short_numeric_rows_are_fragments_and_residual_rows_are_counted() -> None:
    assert is_fragment_row(line("( 4 %", 100)) and is_fragment_row(line(")", 100))
    assert is_fragment_row(line("2021", 100, x=60) + line("2022", 100, x=160) + line("2023", 100, x=260))
    assert not is_fragment_row(line("plain body words number 3 of the paragraph", 100))
    rows = [*body_lines(100, 5), line("40%", 200), line("20%", 200 + LEAD), line("0%", 200 + 2 * LEAD)]
    assert residual_fragment_rows(rows) == 3


def test_caption_and_source_rows_are_recognised() -> None:
    assert is_caption_text("图 3：公司营收结构") and is_caption_text("图表12 毛利率") and is_caption_text("Figure 2. Revenue")
    assert not is_caption_text("图形处理器业务继续增长")


def test_masked_real_chart_page_has_a_figure_region() -> None:
    d = json.loads(Path("tests/fixtures/layouts/r_20250305_taipingyang_p1.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    lay = layout_from_fixture(d)
    assert lay.graphics, "夹具缺 graphics：先按 Step 5 重新生成夹具"
    cs = strip_charts(lay, body_font_size([lay]))
    assert cs.figures and cs.unsafe_reason is None
    assert alnum(w.text for w in lay.words) == alnum(w.text for w in cs.layout.words) + alnum([cs.stripped_text])


def test_real_v1_false_alarm_page_strips_the_bracket_fragments() -> None:
    hits = sorted(RAW_RESEARCH.glob("*_AP202503051644062530.pdf"))
    if not hits:
        pytest.skip("原始研报不入库（docs/rawdata/.gitignore），本机没有这份文件")
    pages = extract_pages(hits[0].read_bytes())
    s = strip_decor(pages)[0]
    cs = strip_charts(s.layout, body_font_size([p.layout for p in strip_decor(pages)]))
    assert cs.figures and "%" in cs.stripped_text  # 带括号的百分比碎片随图表区域剥离，不再进入正文
```

```python
# tests/parse/test_figures.py
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas

from ragdemo.parse import figures as fig_mod
from ragdemo.parse.figures import (
    FIGURE_KEY_PREFIX,
    FigureRef,
    FigureRegion,
    FigureSkip,
    crop_figures,
    figure_from_dict,
    figure_to_dict,
)
from ragdemo_core.blob import LocalBlobStore


class CountingBlob(LocalBlobStore):
    def __init__(self, root: Path) -> None:
        super().__init__(root)
        self.puts = 0

    def put(self, key: str, data: bytes) -> str:
        self.puts += 1
        return super().put(key, data)


def _pdf(size: tuple[float, float] = (595.0, 842.0), rotate: int = 0) -> bytes:
    buf = BytesIO()
    c = canvas.Canvas(buf, pagesize=size)
    if rotate:
        c.setPageRotation(rotate)
    c.setFillColorRGB(0.2, 0.4, 0.8)
    c.rect(60, size[1] - 280, 260, 160, stroke=0, fill=1)
    c.showPage()
    c.save()
    return buf.getvalue()


REGION = FigureRegion(page=1, bbox=(60.0, 120.0, 320.0, 280.0), detection="vector", caption="图1：测试", stripped_words=7)
DOC = hashlib.sha256(b"doc").hexdigest()


def _crop(
    pdf: bytes, blob: LocalBlobStore, sizes: dict[int, tuple[float, float]] | None = None
) -> tuple[list[FigureRef], list[FigureSkip]]:
    return crop_figures(pdf, [REGION], doc_sha256=DOC, blob=blob, source="local", detector_version="chart-v1",
                        page_sizes=sizes or {1: (595.0, 842.0)})


def test_crop_renders_the_region_as_a_content_addressed_png(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    blob = CountingBlob(tmp_path)
    (ref,), skips = _crop(_pdf(), blob)
    assert skips == []
    png = blob.get(ref.image_key)
    assert ref.image_key == f"{FIGURE_KEY_PREFIX}/{DOC}/{hashlib.sha256(png).hexdigest()}.png"
    assert ref.image_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(png).hexdigest() and ref.png_bytes == len(png)
    img = Image.open(BytesIO(png))
    assert img.format == "PNG" and img.size == (ref.width_px, ref.height_px)
    assert ref.dpi == 120 and abs(ref.width_px - (264 * 120 / 72)) <= 2  # 含两侧各 2pt 留白
    assert ref.bbox_pt == pytest.approx((58.0, 118.0, 322.0, 282.0))
    assert ref.bbox_norm == pytest.approx((58 / 595, 118 / 842, 322 / 595, 282 / 842))
    assert (ref.caption, ref.source, ref.detection, ref.stripped_words) == ("图1：测试", "local", "vector", 7)


def test_cropping_twice_is_idempotent(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    blob = CountingBlob(tmp_path)
    first, _ = _crop(_pdf(), blob)
    second, _ = _crop(_pdf(), blob)
    assert first == second and blob.puts == 1


def test_large_regions_are_capped_at_the_long_edge(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    big = FigureRegion(page=1, bbox=(0.0, 0.0, 1190.0, 1684.0), detection="embedded_image")
    (ref,), _ = crop_figures(_pdf((1190.0, 1684.0)), [big], doc_sha256=DOC, blob=LocalBlobStore(tmp_path),
                             source="local", detector_version="chart-v1", page_sizes={1: (1190.0, 1684.0)})
    assert max(ref.width_px, ref.height_px) <= 1600 and ref.dpi < 120


def test_oversized_pngs_fall_back_then_are_skipped_not_stored(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(fig_mod, "FIGURE_MAX_BYTES", 10)
    refs, skips = _crop(_pdf(), LocalBlobStore(tmp_path))
    assert refs == [] and len(skips) == 1 and "上限" in skips[0].reason
    assert not any(tmp_path.rglob("*.png"))


def test_rotated_pages_and_size_mismatches_are_skipped_not_misaligned(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    refs, skips = _crop(_pdf(rotate=90), LocalBlobStore(tmp_path))
    assert refs == [] and "Rotate" in skips[0].reason
    refs, skips = _crop(_pdf(), LocalBlobStore(tmp_path), sizes={1: (612.0, 792.0)})
    assert refs == [] and "尺寸" in skips[0].reason


def test_figure_dicts_round_trip(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (ref,), _ = _crop(_pdf(), LocalBlobStore(tmp_path))
    d = figure_to_dict(ref)
    assert set(d) >= {"doc_sha256", "page", "bbox_pt", "bbox_norm", "image_key", "image_sha256", "width_px",
                      "height_px", "dpi", "png_bytes", "caption", "source_note", "source", "detection",
                      "detector_version", "stripped_words"}
    assert figure_from_dict(d) == ref
```

```python
# tests/parse/local/test_recall_diag.py
from __future__ import annotations

from ragdemo.parse.local.recall_diag import MissingNumbers, classify_missing_numbers


def test_missing_numbers_are_attributed_to_decor_tokenization_or_absent_text_layer() -> None:
    m = classify_missing_numbers(
        "91.2 3 2029 4% 77", "91.2", layer_text="收入 91.2 亿元（同比 (4%)）", stripped_text="第 3 页"
    )
    assert (m.reference_total, m.hit) == (5, 1)
    assert (m.decor_stripped, m.tokenization, m.not_in_text_layer, m.unexplained) == (1, 1, 2, 0)
    assert m.examples == ("4%",)
    assert m.recall == 0.2


def test_numbers_stripped_with_a_figure_region_are_their_own_category() -> None:
    m = classify_missing_numbers("91.2 34.1% 3", "91.2", layer_text="91.2", stripped_text="第 3 页", figure_text="34.1% 2021")
    assert (m.figure_stripped, m.decor_stripped, m.unexplained) == (1, 1, 0)


def test_a_number_the_text_layer_has_to_spare_but_the_output_lacks_is_unexplained() -> None:
    m = classify_missing_numbers("91.2 5", "91.2", layer_text="91.2 5", stripped_text="")
    assert m.unexplained == 1 and m.examples == ("5",)


def test_numbers_already_used_by_the_output_are_not_counted_as_spare() -> None:
    m = classify_missing_numbers("1 1 1", "1", layer_text="1", stripped_text="")
    assert m.unexplained == 0 and m.tokenization == 2


def test_plus_accumulates_and_empty_recall_is_one() -> None:
    a = MissingNumbers(3, 2, 1, 0, 0, 0, ("x",), figure_stripped=1)
    b = MissingNumbers(2, 2, 0, 0, 0, 0, ("y",))
    assert a.plus(b) == MissingNumbers(5, 4, 1, 0, 0, 0, ("x", "y"), figure_stripped=1)
    assert MissingNumbers().recall == 1.0
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 运行确认失败**

Run: `uv run --no-sync pytest tests/parse/local/test_chart.py tests/parse/test_figures.py tests/parse/local/test_recall_diag.py -q -p no:warnings`
Expected: FAIL（`ModuleNotFoundError: ragdemo.parse.local.chart` / `ragdemo.parse.figures` / `recall_diag`；`ImportError: cannot import name 'self_check'`）

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`pipeline.py` 在 `_self_check` 定义之后加一行：`self_check = _self_check  # L0 第二版与测试使用的公开名`。`decor.py` 在 `_body_text` 之后加 `body_text = _body_text  # chart.py 用同一口径重建剥离后的文字层`。

`layout.py`（只追加，不改既有字段与 `Word`）：

```python
GraphicKind = Literal["image", "rect", "line", "curve"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Graphic:
    """页面上的一个非文字对象的外接框（pdfplumber 坐标：原点页面左上角，y 向下，单位 pt）。"""

    kind: GraphicKind
    x0: float
    x1: float
    top: float
    bottom: float
    filled: bool = False  # rect / curve 是否填充（pdfplumber 对象的 fill）
    points: int = 0  # curve 的点数（pdfplumber 的 pts）；其他为 0

    @property
    def width(self) -> float:
        return self.x1 - self.x0

    @property
    def height(self) -> float:
        return self.bottom - self.top


def _graphic(kind: GraphicKind, o: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Graphic:
    return Graphic(
        kind, float(o["x0"]), float(o["x1"]), float(o["top"]), float(o["bottom"]),
        bool(o.get("fill", False)), len(o.get("pts") or []) if kind == "curve" else 0,
    )
```

`PageLayout` 末尾加 `graphics: tuple[Graphic, ...] = ()`；`extract_pages` 构造 `PageLayout` 时传 `graphics=tuple([_graphic("image", o) for o in p.images] + [_graphic("rect", o) for o in p.rects] + [_graphic("line", o) for o in p.lines] + [_graphic("curve", o) for o in p.curves])`（导入区加 `Literal`、`Mapping`）。`decor.strip_decor` 用 `dataclasses.replace` 构造剥离后版面，`graphics` 自动保留。

`samples.py`：`layout_to_fixture` 增加键 `"graphics": [[g.kind, round(g.x0, 2), round(g.x1, 2), round(g.top, 2), round(g.bottom, 2), g.filled, g.points] for g in layout.graphics]`（只有几何，没有任何文字或像素，符合 ADR-0014 的脱敏口径）；`layout_from_fixture` 读 `d.get("graphics", [])` 还原成 `Graphic`（旧夹具无此键时为空元组）。

```python
# packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/figures.py
"""图表区域 → PNG 图片 + 元数据（两条解析路径共用）。

只做"按外接框渲染裁剪、内容寻址存入对象存储、产出元数据"。图片**不送任何模型、不做 OCR**，
图里的数据不被解读（与 parse/textin.py 关闭 apply_image_analysis 同一理由：不越过计算 / 生成分离）。
页面带 /Rotate、或 pdfium 的页面尺寸与版面提取的尺寸不一致（CropBox 偏移）时不裁、记 FigureSkip——
宁可少一张图，也不存一张错位的图。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import hashlib
import io
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Final, Literal

import pypdfium2 as pdfium
from PIL import Image

from ragdemo_core.blob import BlobStore

BBox = tuple[float, float, float, float]  # (x0, top, x1, bottom)，pt，原点页面左上角，x 向右、y 向下
Detection = Literal["embedded_image", "vector"]
FigureSource = Literal["local", "textin"]  # 该文档 / 该页的正文由谁解析；检测与裁图两条路径都是本地检测器

FIGURE_DPI: Final = 120  # 实测：100 dpi 研报均值约 121 KB / 张、150 dpi 约 218 KB；120 dpi 下刻度字可读
FIGURE_FALLBACK_DPI: Final = 96  # 超过体积上限时降到这个 dpi 再试一次
FIGURE_MAX_EDGE_PX: Final = 1600  # 长边上限：A4 整页 842pt@120dpi = 1403 px 不触发，A3 / 超大图按比例降 dpi
FIGURE_MAX_BYTES: Final = 1_000_000  # 单张上限：实测 150 dpi 最大 890 KB，120 dpi 下正常图远低于此
FIGURE_PAD_PT: Final = 2.0
FIGURE_KEY_PREFIX: Final = "parse/figures"  # 与 parse/textin/<hash>/... 并列，在 blob_root（默认 data/blob）下
PAGE_SIZE_TOL_PT: Final = 0.5


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FigureRegion:
    page: int  # 从 1 起
    bbox: BBox
    detection: Detection
    caption: str = ""  # 图注原文（文字层逐字），没有为空
    source_note: str = ""  # "资料来源：…" 原文，没有为空
    stripped_words: int = 0  # 从正文流剥离的词数（内容在 PageResult.stripped_text）


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FigureRef:
    doc_sha256: str  # 原始 PDF 字节的 sha256（= core.document.content_hash）；doc_id 在入库时绑定
    page: int
    bbox_pt: BBox  # 实际裁剪框（含 FIGURE_PAD_PT 留白，已裁到页面内）
    bbox_norm: BBox  # 同一框按页宽高归一化到 [0,1]，与 core.doc_block.bbox 的口径一致（左上原点）
    image_key: str  # 对象存储 key：parse/figures/<doc_sha256>/<image_sha256>.png
    image_sha256: str
    width_px: int
    height_px: int
    dpi: int
    png_bytes: int
    caption: str
    source_note: str
    source: FigureSource
    detection: Detection
    detector_version: str
    stripped_words: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FigureSkip:
    page: int
    bbox: BBox
    reason: str


def figure_key(doc_sha256: str, png: bytes) -> tuple[str, str]:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(png).hexdigest()
    return f"{FIGURE_KEY_PREFIX}/{doc_sha256}/{digest}.png", digest


def _render(page: Any, box: BBox, height: float, width: float, dpi: int) -> Image.Image:
    x0, top, x1, bottom = box
    # pypdfium2 的 crop 是"从四边各裁掉多少"（左、下、右、上，PDF 单位）
    bitmap = page.render(scale=dpi / 72, crop=(x0, height - bottom, width - x1, top))
    image: Image.Image = bitmap.to_pil()
    return image


def _encode(img: Image.Image) -> bytes:
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    img.save(buf, format="PNG", optimize=True)  # 同一像素 → 同一字节（内容寻址的前提）
    return buf.getvalue()


def crop_figures(
    pdf_bytes: bytes,
    regions: Sequence[FigureRegion],
    *,
    doc_sha256: str,
    blob: BlobStore,
    source: FigureSource,
    detector_version: str,
    page_sizes: Mapping[int, tuple[float, float]],
) -> tuple[list[FigureRef], list[FigureSkip]]:
    refs: list[FigureRef] = []
    skips: list[FigureSkip] = []
    pdf = pdfium.PdfDocument(pdf_bytes)
    try:
        for r in regions:
            page = pdf[r.page - 1]
            try:
                width, height = page.get_size()
                if page.get_rotation() % 360 != 0:
                    skips.append(FigureSkip(r.page, r.bbox, "页面带 /Rotate，裁图坐标不可信"))
                    continue
                ew, eh = page_sizes[r.page]
                if abs(width - ew) > PAGE_SIZE_TOL_PT or abs(height - eh) > PAGE_SIZE_TOL_PT:
                    skips.append(FigureSkip(r.page, r.bbox, f"页面尺寸不一致（pdfium {width:.1f}×{height:.1f}，版面 {ew:.1f}×{eh:.1f}）"))
                    continue
                x0, top, x1, bottom = r.bbox
                box = (max(0.0, x0 - FIGURE_PAD_PT), max(0.0, top - FIGURE_PAD_PT),
                       min(width, x1 + FIGURE_PAD_PT), min(height, bottom + FIGURE_PAD_PT))
                if box[2] - box[0] < 1 or box[3] - box[1] < 1:
                    skips.append(FigureSkip(r.page, r.bbox, "区域为空"))
                    continue
                longest = max(box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1])
                dpi = min(FIGURE_DPI, int(FIGURE_MAX_EDGE_PX * 72 / longest))
                img = _render(page, box, height, width, dpi)
                png = _encode(img)
                if len(png) > FIGURE_MAX_BYTES and dpi > FIGURE_FALLBACK_DPI:
                    dpi = FIGURE_FALLBACK_DPI
                    img = _render(page, box, height, width, dpi)
                    png = _encode(img)
                if len(png) > FIGURE_MAX_BYTES:
                    skips.append(FigureSkip(r.page, r.bbox, f"PNG {len(png)} 字节超过上限 {FIGURE_MAX_BYTES}"))
                    continue
                key, digest = figure_key(doc_sha256, png)
                if not blob.exists(key):
                    blob.put(key, png)
                refs.append(FigureRef(
                    doc_sha256, r.page, box, (box[0] / width, box[1] / height, box[2] / width, box[3] / height),
                    key, digest, img.width, img.height, dpi, len(png), r.caption, r.source_note, source,
                    r.detection, detector_version, r.stripped_words,
                ))
            finally:
                page.close()
    finally:
        pdf.close()
    return refs, skips


def figure_to_dict(ref: FigureRef) -> dict[str, object]:
    d = dataclasses.asdict(ref)
    d["bbox_pt"], d["bbox_norm"] = list(ref.bbox_pt), list(ref.bbox_norm)
    return d


def figure_from_dict(d: Mapping[str, Any]) -> FigureRef:
    vals = dict(d)
    vals["bbox_pt"] = tuple(float(x) for x in d["bbox_pt"])
    vals["bbox_norm"] = tuple(float(x) for x in d["bbox_norm"])
    return FigureRef(**vals)
```

```python
# packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/chart.py
"""图表区域识别与图表文字剥离（纯函数，只看几何与文字层）。

图表里的数据不提取（用户 2026-10-13 裁决）：识别区域 → 区域内文字碎片从正文流无损剥离（记入
stripped_text，与页眉页脚同一机制）→ 区域交给 parse/figures.py 裁图入库。表格优先：落在表格网格或
坐标表格区域里的词永不剥离，绘图框里只要有一个表格词，整块区域放弃。闭合校验不做任何豁免。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from typing import Final

from ragdemo.parse.figures import BBox, Detection, FigureRegion
from ragdemo.parse.local.decor import BOTTOM_BAND, TOP_BAND, body_text
from ragdemo.parse.local.layout import Graphic, PageLayout, Word
from ragdemo.parse.local.tables import cluster_rows, find_table_regions, split_segments

CHART_DETECTOR_VERSION: Final = "chart-v1"
# 嵌入图片
IMG_MIN_AREA_SHARE = 0.01
IMG_MIN_SIDE_PT = 36.0
IMG_MAX_AREA_SHARE = 0.60
BAND_MAX_AREA_SHARE = 0.05
SQUARE_MAX_SIDE_PT = 100.0
SQUARE_ASPECT: Final = (0.8, 1.25)
# 矢量图
VECTOR_JOIN_PT = 6.0
VECTOR_MIN_ELEMENTS = 4
VECTOR_MIN_AREA_SHARE = 0.01
RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT = 1.5
MIN_BARS = 3
CURVE_MIN_POINTS = 3
MIN_DIAGONALS = 3
BACKGROUND_AREA_SHARE = 0.25
# 表格网格
GRID_SPAN_SHARE = 0.8
GRID_MIN_H_RULES = 3
GRID_MIN_V_RULES = 2
# 标签与图注
LABEL_MARGIN_EM = 3.0
LABEL_MAX_CHARS = 12
FRAGMENT_MAX_CHARS = 6
FRAGMENT_NUMERIC_SHARE = 0.5
FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP = 8.0
CAPTION_MAX_GAP_EM = 4.0
DEDUP_OVERLAP = 0.8
RESIDUAL_MIN_FRAGMENT_ROWS = 3
REASON_FIGURE: Final = "figure"
REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED: Final = "图表区域内的非水平文字含数字"
REASON_CHART_UNRESOLVED: Final = "图表碎片残留（未检出图表区域）"
_CAPTION = re.compile(r"^(?:图表?|Figure|Fig\.|Chart)\s*[0-9０-９一二三四五六七八九十]+")
_SOURCE_NOTE = re.compile(r"^(?:资料来源|数据来源|来源)\s*[:：]")
_NUMERICISH = frozenset("0123456789.%-+()（）,，％−")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ChartStrip:
    layout: PageLayout  # 剥离图表文字后的正文版面
    figures: tuple[FigureRegion, ...] = ()
    items: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()  # (REASON_FIGURE, 行文字)，与 StrippedPage.items 同形
    discarded: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # 放弃的候选区域与原因（与表格冲突、区域内有正文行）
    unsafe_reason: str | None = None

    @property
    def stripped_text(self) -> str:
        return "\n".join(t for _, t in self.items)

    @property
    def stripped_words(self) -> int:
        return sum(f.stripped_words for f in self.figures)


def _compact(text: str) -> str:
    return "".join(text.split())


def _area(b: BBox) -> float:
    return max(0.0, b[2] - b[0]) * max(0.0, b[3] - b[1])


def _union(boxes: Sequence[BBox]) -> BBox:
    return (min(b[0] for b in boxes), min(b[1] for b in boxes), max(b[2] for b in boxes), max(b[3] for b in boxes))


def _gbox(g: Graphic) -> BBox:
    return (g.x0, g.top, g.x1, g.bottom)


def _wbox(w: Word) -> BBox:
    return (w.x0, w.top, w.x1, w.bottom)


def _center_in(w: Word, b: BBox) -> bool:
    cx = (w.x0 + w.x1) / 2
    return b[0] <= cx <= b[2] and b[1] <= w.mid_y <= b[3]


def _contains(outer: BBox, inner: BBox) -> bool:
    return outer[0] <= inner[0] and outer[1] <= inner[1] and inner[2] <= outer[2] and inner[3] <= outer[3]


def _overlap_share(a: BBox, b: BBox) -> float:
    inter = (max(a[0], b[0]), max(a[1], b[1]), min(a[2], b[2]), min(a[3], b[3]))
    return _area(inter) / _area(a) if _area(a) else 0.0


def _in_bands(b: BBox, height: float) -> bool:
    return b[3] <= TOP_BAND * height or b[1] >= (1 - BOTTOM_BAND) * height


def _is_rule(g: Graphic) -> bool:
    return g.kind in ("line", "rect") and min(g.width, g.height) <= RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT


def _cluster(items: Sequence[Graphic]) -> list[list[Graphic]]:
    """外扩 VECTOR_JOIN_PT 后外接框相交即连通（按 top 排序的扫描线 + 并查集）。"""
    order = sorted(range(len(items)), key=lambda i: items[i].top)
    parent = list(range(len(items)))

    def find(i: int) -> int:
        while parent[i] != i:
            parent[i] = parent[parent[i]]
            i = parent[i]
        return i

    for pos, i in enumerate(order):
        gi = items[i]
        for j in order[pos + 1:]:
            gj = items[j]
            if gj.top > gi.bottom + VECTOR_JOIN_PT:
                break
            if gj.x0 <= gi.x1 + VECTOR_JOIN_PT and gi.x0 <= gj.x1 + VECTOR_JOIN_PT:
                parent[find(j)] = find(i)
    groups: dict[int, list[Graphic]] = {}
    for i, g in enumerate(items):
        groups.setdefault(find(i), []).append(g)
    return list(groups.values())


def _marks(cluster: Sequence[Graphic], box: BBox) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """(柱, 曲线 / 折线, 斜线)。大面积填充（底色面板）不算标记。"""
    bg = BACKGROUND_AREA_SHARE * _area(box)
    bars = sum(1 for g in cluster if g.kind == "rect" and g.filled and not _is_rule(g) and g.width * g.height < bg)
    curves = sum(1 for g in cluster if g.kind == "curve" and g.points >= CURVE_MIN_POINTS
                 and not (g.filled and g.width * g.height >= bg))
    diagonals = sum(1 for g in cluster if g.kind == "line" and min(g.width, g.height) > RULE_MAX_THICKNESS_PT)
    return bars, curves, diagonals


def embedded_image_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], width: float, height: float) -> list[BBox]:
    page_area = width * height
    out: list[BBox] = []
    for g in graphics:
        if g.kind != "image" or g.width <= 0 or g.height <= 0:
            continue
        share = g.width * g.height / page_area
        if share < IMG_MIN_AREA_SHARE or min(g.width, g.height) < IMG_MIN_SIDE_PT:
            continue  # 图标
        if share > IMG_MAX_AREA_SHARE:
            continue  # 整页底图 / 水印
        if _in_bands(_gbox(g), height) and share < BAND_MAX_AREA_SHARE:
            continue  # 页眉页脚 logo
        aspect = g.width / g.height
        if max(g.width, g.height) <= SQUARE_MAX_SIDE_PT and SQUARE_ASPECT[0] <= aspect <= SQUARE_ASPECT[1]:
            continue  # 二维码 / 方形 logo
        out.append(_gbox(g))
    return out


def grid_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]], height: float) -> list[BBox]:
    """表格网格：只由细线组成、贯通横线 >= 3 条且贯通竖线 >= 2 条、框内无绘图标记、框内 >= 2 行多段文字。"""
    rules = [g for g in graphics if _is_rule(g) and not _in_bands(_gbox(g), height)]
    marks = [g for g in graphics if g.kind != "image" and not _is_rule(g)]
    out: list[BBox] = []
    for c in _cluster(rules):
        box = _union([_gbox(g) for g in c])
        w, h = box[2] - box[0], box[3] - box[1]
        hs = {round(g.top) for g in c if g.width >= GRID_SPAN_SHARE * w and g.width > g.height}
        vs = {round(g.x0) for g in c if g.height >= GRID_SPAN_SHARE * h and g.height > g.width}
        if len(hs) < GRID_MIN_H_RULES or len(vs) < GRID_MIN_V_RULES:
            continue
        if any(_contains(box, _gbox(m)) and _marks([m], box) != (0, 0, 0) for m in marks):
            continue  # 框内有柱 / 线：这是带边框的图表，不是表格
        inside = [[w_ for w_ in r if _center_in(w_, box)] for r in rows]
        if sum(1 for r in inside if len(split_segments(r, FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP)) >= 2) >= 2:
            out.append(box)
    return out


def plot_boxes(graphics: Sequence[Graphic], width: float, height: float, grids: Sequence[BBox]) -> list[BBox]:
    elems = [g for g in graphics if g.kind != "image" and not _in_bands(_gbox(g), height)
             and not any(_contains(gb, _gbox(g)) for gb in grids)]
    out: list[BBox] = []
    for c in _cluster(elems):
        box = _union([_gbox(g) for g in c])
        if len(c) < VECTOR_MIN_ELEMENTS or _area(box) < VECTOR_MIN_AREA_SHARE * width * height:
            continue
        bars, curves, diagonals = _marks(c, box)
        if bars >= MIN_BARS or curves >= 1 or diagonals >= MIN_DIAGONALS:
            out.append(box)
    return out


def is_caption_text(text: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_CAPTION.match(text.strip()))


def is_source_note(text: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_SOURCE_NOTE.match(text.strip()))


def _row_text(row: Sequence[Word]) -> str:
    return " ".join(w.text for w in row)


def is_fragment_row(row: Sequence[Word]) -> bool:
    """整行很短且以数字类字符为主（"40%" "( 4 %" ")"），或一行里 >= 3 段短词且过半含数字（刻度行）。"""
    text = _compact("".join(w.text for w in row))
    if not text:
        return False
    if len(text) <= FRAGMENT_MAX_CHARS and sum(ch in _NUMERICISH for ch in text) >= FRAGMENT_NUMERIC_SHARE * len(text):
        return True
    segs = [_compact("".join(w.text for w in s)) for s in split_segments(row, FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP)]
    if len(segs) < 3:
        return False
    return all(len(s) <= FRAGMENT_MAX_CHARS for s in segs) and 2 * sum(any(ch.isdigit() for ch in s) for s in segs) >= len(segs)


def residual_fragment_rows(rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]]) -> int:
    """剥离后仍留在正文里的碎片行数：>= RESIDUAL_MIN_FRAGMENT_ROWS 说明有图表没检出（T5 判 chart_unresolved）。"""
    return sum(1 for r in rows if is_fragment_row(r))


def _is_label_row(row: Sequence[Word]) -> bool:
    return all(len(_compact("".join(w.text for w in s))) <= LABEL_MAX_CHARS for s in split_segments(row, FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP))


def _nearest(rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]], box: BBox, gap: float, below: bool) -> str:
    best: tuple[float, str] | None = None
    for r in rows:
        x0, x1 = min(w.x0 for w in r), max(w.x1 for w in r)
        top, bottom = min(w.top for w in r), max(w.bottom for w in r)
        if x1 < box[0] or x0 > box[2]:
            continue
        d = top - box[3] if below else box[1] - bottom
        if -2.0 <= d <= gap and (best is None or d < best[0]):
            best = (d, _row_text(r))
    return best[1] if best else ""


def strip_charts(layout: PageLayout, body_size: float) -> ChartStrip:
    em = body_size or 10.0
    words = layout.words
    upright_rows = cluster_rows([w for w in words if w.upright])
    caption_rows = [r for r in upright_rows if is_caption_text(_row_text(r))]
    source_rows = [r for r in upright_rows if is_source_note(_row_text(r))]
    keep = {id(w) for r in (*caption_rows, *source_rows) for w in r}
    grids = grid_boxes(layout.graphics, upright_rows, layout.height)
    candidates: list[tuple[BBox, Detection]] = []
    candidates.extend((b, "embedded_image") for b in embedded_image_boxes(layout.graphics, layout.width, layout.height))
    candidates.extend((b, "vector") for b in plot_boxes(layout.graphics, layout.width, layout.height, grids))
    protected = set(keep) | {id(w) for w in words for g in grids if _center_in(w, g)}
    for reg in find_table_regions(upright_rows):
        # 坐标表格的词受保护；落在候选绘图框内部的词除外——那是图表自己的数据标签，
        # 只是被 cluster_rows 与旁边的表格行聚到了同一行（图表紧邻表格时常见）
        protected |= {id(w) for r in reg for w in r if not any(_center_in(w, c) for c, _ in candidates)}
    taken: set[int] = set()
    accepted: list[BBox] = []
    figures: list[FigureRegion] = []
    discarded: list[str] = []
    unsafe: str | None = None
    for box, detection in candidates:
        if any(_overlap_share(box, a) >= DEDUP_OVERLAP for a in accepted):
            continue
        inner = [w for w in words if _center_in(w, box) and id(w) not in taken]
        if any(id(w) in protected and id(w) not in keep for w in inner):
            discarded.append(f"{detection} {tuple(round(v) for v in box)}：绘图框内有表格词，放弃（不剥任何字）")
            continue
        inner_rows = cluster_rows([w for w in inner if w.upright and id(w) not in keep])
        if not all(_is_label_row(r) for r in inner_rows):
            discarded.append(f"{detection} {tuple(round(v) for v in box)}：绘图框内有正文行，不是图表")
            continue
        m = LABEL_MARGIN_EM * em
        outer = (box[0] - m, box[1] - m, box[2] + m, box[3] + m)
        labels: list[Word] = []
        for r in upright_rows:
            for seg in split_segments(r, FRAGMENT_SEGMENT_GAP):
                if (all(_center_in(w, outer) and not _center_in(w, box) for w in seg)
                        and len(_compact("".join(w.text for w in seg))) <= LABEL_MAX_CHARS
                        and not any(id(w) in protected or id(w) in taken for w in seg)):
                    labels.extend(seg)
        labels += [w for w in words if not w.upright and _center_in(w, outer) and not _center_in(w, box)
                   and id(w) not in taken and len(_compact(w.text)) <= LABEL_MAX_CHARS]
        strip = [w for w in [*inner, *labels] if id(w) not in keep]
        if any(not w.upright and any(ch.isdigit() for ch in w.text) for w in strip):
            unsafe = REASON_FIGURE_ROTATED
        taken |= {id(w) for w in strip}
        region = _union([box, *(_wbox(w) for w in strip)])
        gap = CAPTION_MAX_GAP_EM * em
        caption = _nearest(caption_rows, region, gap, below=False) or _nearest(caption_rows, region, gap, below=True)
        figures.append(FigureRegion(layout.page, region, detection, caption,
                                    _nearest(source_rows, region, gap, below=True), len(strip)))
        accepted.append(box)
    if not taken:
        return ChartStrip(layout, tuple(figures), (), tuple(discarded), unsafe)
    gone = [w for w in words if id(w) in taken]
    items = tuple((REASON_FIGURE, _row_text(r)) for r in cluster_rows([w for w in gone if w.upright]))
    items += tuple((REASON_FIGURE, w.text) for w in gone if not w.upright)
    kept = [w for w in words if id(w) not in taken]
    return ChartStrip(replace(layout, words=kept, text=body_text(kept)), tuple(figures), items, tuple(discarded), unsafe)
```

`recall_diag.py`（与原计划相同，追加图表一类）：

```python
# packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/recall_diag.py
"""数字召回的缺失归因（纯函数）：参考（TextIn）里有、候选（L0）里没有的数字，各自为什么没有。

- figure_stripped：随图表区域一起剥离的文字里（图表数据不提取，用户 2026-10-13 裁决）；
- decor_stripped：在被剥离的装饰文字（页眉页脚 / 页码 / 旋转字）里；
- unexplained：文字层里还有富余（没被输出用掉）的同一个数字，输出却少了——构造上不可能，出现即 bug；
- not_in_text_layer：数字串在文字层里根本不存在（TextIn 从图片里 OCR 的）；
- tokenization：数字串在文字层里，但分词不同（跨词、与相邻字符连写等），逐个列入 examples 供人工复核。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass

from ragdemo.parse.local.number_closure import fold_text, numbers_in

_NON_DIGIT = re.compile(r"\D")


def _digits(text: str) -> str:
    return _NON_DIGIT.sub("", fold_text(text))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MissingNumbers:
    reference_total: int = 0
    hit: int = 0
    decor_stripped: int = 0
    tokenization: int = 0
    not_in_text_layer: int = 0
    unexplained: int = 0
    examples: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # tokenization 与 unexplained 的数字
    figure_stripped: int = 0  # 追加在末尾：位置参数构造不受影响

    @property
    def recall(self) -> float:
        return self.hit / self.reference_total if self.reference_total else 1.0

    def plus(self, other: MissingNumbers) -> MissingNumbers:
        return MissingNumbers(
            self.reference_total + other.reference_total,
            self.hit + other.hit,
            self.decor_stripped + other.decor_stripped,
            self.tokenization + other.tokenization,
            self.not_in_text_layer + other.not_in_text_layer,
            self.unexplained + other.unexplained,
            self.examples + other.examples,
            self.figure_stripped + other.figure_stripped,
        )


def classify_missing_numbers(
    reference: str, candidate: str, *, layer_text: str, stripped_text: str, figure_text: str = ""
) -> MissingNumbers:
    ref, cand = numbers_in(reference), numbers_in(candidate)
    figure = numbers_in(figure_text)
    stripped = numbers_in(stripped_text)
    spare = numbers_in(layer_text) - cand  # 文字层里没被输出用掉的数字
    layer_digits = _digits(layer_text + "\n" + stripped_text + "\n" + figure_text)
    counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
    examples: list[str] = []
    for n, c in sorted((ref - cand).items()):
        for _ in range(c):
            if figure[n] > 0:
                figure[n] -= 1
                counts["figure"] += 1
            elif stripped[n] > 0:
                stripped[n] -= 1
                counts["decor"] += 1
            elif spare[n] > 0:
                spare[n] -= 1
                counts["unexplained"] += 1
                examples.append(n)
            elif _digits(n) not in layer_digits:
                counts["absent"] += 1
            else:
                counts["token"] += 1
                examples.append(n)
    hit = sum(min(c, cand.get(n, 0)) for n, c in ref.items())
    return MissingNumbers(
        sum(ref.values()), hit, counts["decor"], counts["token"], counts["absent"], counts["unexplained"],
        tuple(sorted(set(examples))), counts["figure"],
    )
```

`scripts/l0v2.py` 追加 `diag-v1`（导入区加 `from ragdemo.parse.local.compare import number_recall_precision, plain_text_from_markdown` 与 `from ragdemo.parse.local.recall_diag import MissingNumbers, classify_missing_numbers`）：

```python
V1_DOCS_TSV: Final = Path("data/experiments/parse-compare-det-docs-2026-10-12.tsv")
V1_MD_DIR: Final = Path("data/experiments/det-md")


def cmd_diag_v1() -> None:
    """复现并解释 v1 的数字召回均值 0.9855：候选用 v1 运行留在本机的逐页 markdown，口径与 v1 完全相同
    （整份文档、只算 accepted 文档、参考 = 整份 TextIn markdown 去标签）。v1 没有图表剥离，figure_text 为空。"""
    blob = LocalBlobStore(load_config().blob_root)
    docs = _db_docs()
    rows = [ln.split("\t") for ln in V1_DOCS_TSV.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
    head = rows[0]
    col = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(head)}
    out = OUT_ROOT / "diag-v1-recall.tsv"
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    total = MissingNumbers()
    recalls: list[float] = []
    lines = ["doc_id\tdoc_type\tv1_recall\trecomputed\tref\thit\tdecor\ttokenization\tnot_in_text_layer\tunexplained\texamples"]
    for r in rows[1:]:
        if r[col["accepted"]] != "1":
            continue
        doc_id = r[col["doc_id"]]
        raw_ref, _json_ref, md_ref, doc_type = docs[doc_id]
        pages = sorted((V1_MD_DIR / doc_id).glob("p*.md"))
        candidate = "\n\n".join(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") for p in pages)
        reference = plain_text_from_markdown(blob.get(md_ref).decode("utf-8"))
        stripped = strip_decor(extract_pages(blob.get(raw_ref)))
        m = classify_missing_numbers(
            reference, candidate,
            layer_text="\n".join(s.layout.text for s in stripped),
            stripped_text="\n".join(s.stripped_text for s in stripped),
        )
        recomputed = number_recall_precision(reference, candidate)[0]
        if abs(recomputed - float(r[col["num_recall"]])) > 1e-4:
            raise SystemExit(f"{doc_id}：复算召回 {recomputed:.4f} ≠ v1 记录 {r[col['num_recall']]}，v1 产物已变，停止")
        total = total.plus(m)
        recalls.append(recomputed)
        lines.append("\t".join([
            doc_id, doc_type, r[col["num_recall"]], f"{recomputed:.4f}", str(m.reference_total), str(m.hit),
            str(m.decor_stripped), str(m.tokenization), str(m.not_in_text_layer), str(m.unexplained),
            " ".join(m.examples)[:300],
        ]))
    out.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"{out}\naccepted {len(recalls)} 份，召回均值 {sum(recalls) / len(recalls):.4f}")
    print(f"缺失合计：decor {total.decor_stripped}、tokenization {total.tokenization}、"
          f"not_in_text_layer {total.not_in_text_layer}、unexplained {total.unexplained}")
```

`main()` 里加 `sub.add_parser("diag-v1")` 与分支 `elif args.cmd == "diag-v1": cmd_diag_v1()`。

`cmd_multicol_eval`（T3 调参期间新增）改为与 T5 `parse_layouts_l0` 同一次序判定，并加 `--charts {on,off}`（默认 `on`；`off` 复现 T3 的口径用于对比）。把其中 `run()` 的取数换成：

```python
def _column_flags(pdf: bytes, *, charts: bool) -> dict[int, bool]:
    """剥装饰 → （charts 时）剥图表区域 → 整页多栏判定。与 parse_layouts_l0 同一次序。"""
    stripped = strip_decor(extract_pages(pdf))
    body = body_font_size([s.layout for s in stripped])
    out: dict[int, bool] = {}
    for s in stripped:
        lay = strip_charts(s.layout, body).layout if charts else s.layout
        out[s.layout.page] = page_columns(lay, body).multicolumn or any(not w.upright for w in lay.words)
    return out
```

（`EvalRow` 的预测列改为 `_column_flags(...)[lb.page]`；公告页 `layout_unsupported` 占比那一段改为统计 `_column_flags` 为真的页占比，打印口径名改为"判多栏页占比（G11 类比）"。导入 `strip_charts`、`page_columns`、`body_font_size`。）

### Task 5：页型路由接口（`parse_layouts_l0`）、评估运行器、混合成本与门槛

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/l0.py`；`packages/ragdemo/src/ragdemo/parse/local/l0_eval.py`（T3 多栏调参期间已建并含 `EvalRow` / `aggregate_multicol` / `parse_labels` / `format_report`——保留它们，本任务**追加**下面的内容）
- Modify: `scripts/l0v2.py`（新增 `run`、`heading-sheet`、`spot-sheet`、`figure-sheet`、`gates`）
- Create（由脚本生成模板，用户填写后入库）: `docs/10.12-plan/l0v2/figure-labels.tsv`
- Test: `tests/parse/local/test_l0.py`、`tests/parse/local/test_l0_eval.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PageLayout`、`extract_pages`；`strip_decor`；`needs_handback`、`PageResult`、`self_check`（pipeline）；`typeset_document`、`body_font_size`（typeset）；`cluster_rows`、`find_table_regions`、`split_segments`（tables）；`detect_columns`（T3）；`strip_charts`、`ChartStrip`、`residual_fragment_rows`、`RESIDUAL_MIN_FRAGMENT_ROWS`、`REASON_CHART_UNRESOLVED`、`CHART_DETECTOR_VERSION`（T4）；`FigureRegion`、`crop_figures`（`ragdemo.parse.figures`，T4）；`is_financial_statement_page`（`ragdemo.parse.tiered.classify`，只依赖 `number_closure`，无循环导入）；`numbers_in`；`blocks_from_detail(detail, *, page_dims)`（`ragdemo.parse.textin`）；`table_data_rows`、`heading_matches_v2`、`HeadingMatchV2`、`plain_text_from_markdown`（compare）；`MissingNumbers`、`classify_missing_numbers`（T4）；`read_split`、`SampleSet`（T1）。
- Produces（T6–T8 与分级路由计划修订使用）：
  - l0：`PageKind = Literal["plain_text", "table", "multicolumn", "chart_unresolved", "financial_statement", "no_text_layer"]`；`L0_VERSION: Final = "det-v2"`；`TABLELIKE_GAP_EM = 2.0`；`TABLELIKE_MIN_ROWS = 2`；`REASON_ROTATED: Final = "多栏版面：旋转文字残留"`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) PageClass(kind: PageKind, reason: str = "")`；`tablelike_run(rows: Sequence[Sequence[Word]], body_size: float) -> int`；`classify_page(layout: PageLayout, *, original_text: str, body_size: float, charts: ChartStrip | None = None) -> PageClass`（`layout` = 剥装饰后的版面；`charts` 缺省时内部调用 `strip_charts(layout, body_size)`）；`@dataclass(frozen=True) L0Page(page: int, kind: PageKind, result: PageResult, figures: tuple[FigureRegion, ...] = (), body: PageLayout | None = None, figure_text: str = "")`（属性 `accepted: bool`；`figures` 对每个有文字层页都给出——非接受页也给，供 L1 页裁图；`body` = 剥离装饰与图表文字后的正文版面，审计用；`figure_text` = 被剥的图表文字，也已并入 `result.stripped_text`；三个新字段都有缺省值，T7 路由测试里的 `L0Page(1, kind, PageResult(...))` 构造不受影响）；`parse_layouts_l0(pages: Sequence[PageLayout]) -> list[L0Page]`；`parse_pdf_l0(pdf_bytes: bytes) -> list[L0Page]`
  - l0_eval：`C_TEXTIN_MEASURED: Final = 34.84 / 257`；`textin_page_markdowns(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[int, str]`（**不含 `figure` 块**）；`textin_page_tables(payload: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[int, str]`（每页 TextIn 表格块 markdown，G13b 用）；`@dataclass(frozen=True) FigureLabelTotals(pages: int = 0, charts_total: int = 0, charts_covered: int = 0, boxes: int = 0, boxes_chart: int = 0, boxes_table_or_body: int = 0)`（属性 `recall`、`precision`）；`read_figure_labels(path: Path, sample_set: str) -> FigureLabelTotals`；`content_audit(markdown: str, words: Sequence[str]) -> bool`；`page_has_table_rows(reference_md: str) -> bool`；`Label = Literal["single", "multi", "unsure"]`；`PageKey = tuple[str, str, int]`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) MulticolRates(n_multi: int, missed: int, n_single: int, false_positive: int)`（属性 `miss_rate`、`fp_rate`）；`multicolumn_rates(labels: Mapping[PageKey, Label], kinds: Mapping[PageKey, str], accepted: Mapping[PageKey, bool]) -> MulticolRates`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) HeadingTotals(matched: int = 0, cand_total: int = 0, ref_total: int = 0, rel_agree: int = 0, rel_total: int = 0)`（方法 `add(m: HeadingMatchV2) -> HeadingTotals`，属性 `recall`、`precision`、`relative_level_agreement`）；`CostKind = Literal["l0_accepted", "table", "multicolumn", "chart_unresolved", "closure_failed", "financial_statement", "no_text_layer"]`；`COST_KINDS: Final[tuple[CostKind, ...]]`；`cost_kind(p: L0Page) -> CostKind`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) Prices(c_textin: float, c_local: float = 0.0, l1_enabled: bool = False, l1_pass: float = 0.0)`（方法 `downstream() -> float`）；`cost_breakdown(counts: Mapping[CostKind, int], p: Prices) -> dict[CostKind, float]`；`blended_cost_per_page(counts: Mapping[CostKind, int], p: Prices) -> float`；`routing_cost_inputs(counts: Mapping[CostKind, int]) -> tuple[float, float, float]`；`GATES: Final[Mapping[str, float]]`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) Gate(gid: str, name: str, value: float | None, threshold: float, op: str, passed: bool)`；`@dataclass(frozen=True) L0Metrics(...)`（字段见代码）；`evaluate_gates(m: L0Metrics, *, final: bool) -> list[Gate]`；`is_go(gates: Sequence[Gate]) -> bool`；`claim_holdout(lock: Path, *, git_sha: str, today: date) -> None`；`read_multicol_labels(path: Path, sample_set: str) -> dict[PageKey, Label]`；`read_judgments(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]`；`read_spotcheck(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]`；`sample_items(items: Sequence[T], n: int, seed: int) -> list[T]`
  - 脚本：`run --set tune|holdout`（holdout 写锁；裁图写 `data/experiments/l0v2/figure-blob/`，逐图写 `<run>/figures.tsv`）、`heading-sheet --run <目录>`、`spot-sheet --run <目录>`、`figure-sheet --run <目录> --set tune|holdout [--extra]`、`gates --run <目录> --set tune|holdout`

```python
# tests/parse/local/test_l0.py
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from ragdemo.parse.local import l0 as l0_mod
from ragdemo.parse.local.l0 import REASON_ROTATED, L0Page, parse_layouts_l0, parse_pdf_l0, tablelike_run
from ragdemo.parse.local.layout import PageLayout, Word
from ragdemo.parse.local.samples import layout_from_fixture
from ragdemo.parse.local.typeset import TypesetResult
from tests.parse.local.test_chart import TABLE, bar_chart, bar_page, body, pdf_page, plain_table
from tests.parse.local.test_typeset import LEAD, W, H, body_lines, line, page

RAW_RESEARCH = Path("docs/rawdata/688041-hygon/research")


def _plain(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    return page(*body_lines(100, 12), n=n)


def _table(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    rows = [line(a, 300 + i * LEAD, x=60) + line(b, 300 + i * LEAD, x=260) + line(c, 300 + i * LEAD, x=420)
            for i, (a, b, c) in enumerate([("Item", "2024", "2023"), ("Revenue", "91.2", "60.1"),
                                           ("Cost", "40.5", "30.2"), ("Profit", "50.7", "29.9")])]
    return page(*body_lines(100, 6), *rows, n=n)


def _two_columns(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    rows = [line(f"left column text row {i} here", 100 + i * LEAD, x=60)
            + line(f"right column text row {i} there", 100 + i * LEAD, x=330) for i in range(12)]
    return page(*rows, n=n)


def _statement(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    rows = [line("合并资产负债表", 100, cjk=True)]
    rows += [line(f"item{i} {i},123.45 {i},678.90", 120 + i * LEAD) for i in range(12)]
    return page(*rows, n=n)


def _chart(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    return page(*body_lines(100, 6), line("40%", 300), line("20%", 300 + LEAD), line("0%", 300 + 2 * LEAD), n=n)


def _rotated(n: int) -> PageLayout:
    p = _plain(n)
    rot = Word("TIALNEDIFNOC", 10, 20, 100, 300, False, 10.0, False)
    return PageLayout(n, W, H, [*p.words, rot], p.text + "\nTIALNEDIFNOC")


def test_page_kinds_route_only_plain_text_pages_through_the_typesetter() -> None:
    # _chart 只有碎片行、没有任何图形对象：检测器找不到区域 → 检测失败 → chart_unresolved 降级
    pages = [_plain(1), _table(2), _two_columns(3), _statement(4), PageLayout(5, W, H, [], ""), _chart(6)]
    out = parse_layouts_l0(pages)
    assert [p.kind for p in out] == [
        "plain_text", "table", "multicolumn", "financial_statement", "no_text_layer", "chart_unresolved",
    ]
    assert [p.accepted for p in out] == [True, False, False, False, False, False]
    assert [p.result.status for p in out] == [
        "ok", "layout_unsupported", "layout_unsupported", "layout_unsupported", "no_text_layer", "layout_unsupported",
    ]
    assert out[0].result.markdown and all(not p.result.markdown for p in out[1:])


def test_rotated_words_left_by_decor_stripping_make_the_page_unsupported() -> None:
    # < 4 页的文档 strip_decor 不剥旋转字（>= 4 页时旋转字被剥进 stripped_text，不会留到这里）
    (p,) = parse_layouts_l0([_rotated(1)])
    assert p.kind == "multicolumn" and p.result.reason.endswith(REASON_ROTATED) and not p.accepted


def test_two_consecutive_gapped_rows_with_numbers_make_a_table_page() -> None:
    rows = [line("营业收入", 300 + i * LEAD, cjk=True) + line("91.2 60.1", 300 + i * LEAD, x=300) for i in range(2)]
    assert tablelike_run(rows, 10.0) == 2
    (p,) = parse_layouts_l0([page(*body_lines(100, 6), *rows)])
    assert p.kind == "table" and "疑似表格行" in p.result.reason


def test_a_typesetter_bug_on_a_plain_page_is_closure_failed_not_accepted(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    real = l0_mod.typeset_document

    def corrupt(layouts: list[PageLayout]) -> list[TypesetResult]:
        return [TypesetResult(r.status, r.markdown.replace("3", "8"), r.reason, r.tables) for r in real(layouts)]

    monkeypatch.setattr(l0_mod, "typeset_document", corrupt)
    (p,) = parse_layouts_l0([_plain(1)])
    assert p.kind == "plain_text" and p.result.status == "closure_failed" and not p.accepted


def test_accepted_markdown_keeps_every_content_character_of_the_text_layer() -> None:
    (p,) = parse_layouts_l0([_plain(1)])
    from collections import Counter

    content = Counter(ch for ch in p.result.markdown if ch.isalnum())
    assert content == Counter(ch for w in _plain(1).words for ch in w.text if ch.isalnum())


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("name", "kind"), [
    ("r_20250424_minsheng_p1", "multicolumn"), ("r_20240819_taipingyang_p1", "multicolumn"),
    ("r_20260415_shanxi_p1", "multicolumn"), ("r_20250807_kaiyuan_p1", "multicolumn"),
])
def test_masked_real_sidebar_pages_are_never_accepted(name: str, kind: str) -> None:
    lay = layout_from_fixture(json.loads(Path(f"tests/fixtures/layouts/{name}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    (p,) = parse_layouts_l0([lay])
    assert p.kind == kind and not p.accepted


def test_a_plain_page_with_a_detected_chart_is_accepted_and_carries_the_figure() -> None:
    pdf = pdf_page(bar_page)
    (p,) = parse_pdf_l0(pdf)
    assert p.kind == "plain_text" and p.accepted
    (fig,) = p.figures
    assert fig.caption == "图1：营业收入与增速" and "图1：营业收入与增速" in p.result.markdown
    assert "40%" not in p.result.markdown and "40%" in p.figure_text and "40%" in p.result.stripped_text
    assert p.body is not None and all(w.text != "40%" for w in p.body.words)


def test_a_chart_directly_above_a_table_still_makes_a_table_page_with_every_table_word_kept() -> None:
    pdf = pdf_page(lambda c: (body(c, 80, 2), bar_chart(c, 80, 120), plain_table(c, 284)))
    (p,) = parse_pdf_l0(pdf)
    assert p.kind == "table" and not p.accepted and len(p.figures) == 1
    assert p.body is not None
    kept = {w.text for w in p.body.words}
    assert all(cell in kept for row in TABLE for cell in row)


def test_real_v1_closure_false_alarm_page_is_routed_away_before_typesetting() -> None:
    hits = sorted(RAW_RESEARCH.glob("*_AP202503051644062530.pdf"))
    if not hits:
        pytest.skip("原始研报不入库（docs/rawdata/.gitignore），本机没有这份文件")
    first: L0Page = parse_pdf_l0(hits[0].read_bytes())[0]
    assert first.kind != "plain_text" and first.result.status != "closure_failed"
```

```python
# tests/parse/local/test_l0_eval.py
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from ragdemo.parse.local.compare import HeadingMatchV2
from ragdemo.parse.local.l0_eval import (
    C_TEXTIN_MEASURED,
    HeadingTotals,
    L0Metrics,
    MulticolRates,
    Prices,
    blended_cost_per_page,
    claim_holdout,
    content_audit,
    cost_breakdown,
    evaluate_gates,
    is_go,
    multicolumn_rates,
    page_has_table_rows,
    read_judgments,
    read_multicol_labels,
    read_spotcheck,
    routing_cost_inputs,
    FigureLabelTotals,
    read_figure_labels,
    sample_items,
    textin_page_markdowns,
    textin_page_tables,
)

COUNTS = {"l0_accepted": 50, "table": 20, "multicolumn": 10, "chart_unresolved": 10,
          "closure_failed": 0, "financial_statement": 5, "no_text_layer": 5}


def test_textin_pages_rebuild_titles_tables_and_drop_vendor_headers() -> None:
    payload = {"result": {"detail": [
        {"page_id": 0, "type": "paragraph", "text": "页眉 688041", "content": 1},
        {"page_id": 0, "type": "paragraph", "text": "一、本期业绩预告情况", "outline_level": 1},
        {"page_id": 0, "type": "paragraph", "text": "营业收入 91.2 亿元"},
        {"page_id": 0, "type": "image", "sub_type": "chart", "text": "40% 30% 2021 2022"},
        {"page_id": 1, "type": "table", "cells": [
            {"row": 0, "col": 0, "text": "项目"}, {"row": 0, "col": 1, "text": "2024"},
            {"row": 1, "col": 0, "text": "收入"}, {"row": 1, "col": 1, "text": "91.2"}]},
    ]}}
    pages = textin_page_markdowns(payload)
    assert pages[1] == "## 一、本期业绩预告情况\n\n营业收入 91.2 亿元"  # figure 块不进参考
    assert pages[2].splitlines()[0] == "| 项目 | 2024 |"
    assert textin_page_tables(payload) == {2: pages[2]}


def test_content_audit_compares_content_character_multisets() -> None:
    assert content_audit("# 收入 91.2\n\n亿元", ["收入", "91.2", "亿元"])
    assert not content_audit("收入 91.3 亿元", ["收入", "91.2", "亿元"])


def test_page_has_table_rows_uses_the_data_row_rule() -> None:
    assert page_has_table_rows("| 项目 | 2024 | 2023 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 收入 | 91.2 | 60.1 |")
    assert not page_has_table_rows("| 项目 | 2024 |\n|---|---|\n| 收入 | 91.2 |")  # 数据行只有 1 个数字


def test_multicolumn_rates_count_accepted_multi_pages_as_misses() -> None:
    labels = {("r", "A", 1): "multi", ("r", "B", 1): "multi", ("r", "C", 1): "single",
              ("r", "D", 1): "single", ("r", "E", 1): "unsure"}
    kinds = {k: v for k, v in [(("r", "A", 1), "multicolumn"), (("r", "B", 1), "plain_text"),
                               (("r", "C", 1), "multicolumn"), (("r", "D", 1), "plain_text"),
                               (("r", "E", 1), "plain_text")]}
    accepted = {("r", "A", 1): False, ("r", "B", 1): True, ("r", "C", 1): False,
                ("r", "D", 1): True, ("r", "E", 1): True}
    m = multicolumn_rates(labels, kinds, accepted)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert m == MulticolRates(n_multi=2, missed=1, n_single=2, false_positive=1)
    assert (m.miss_rate, m.fp_rate) == (0.5, 0.5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="没有运行结果"):
        multicolumn_rates({("r", "Z", 9): "multi"}, kinds, accepted)


def test_heading_totals_are_micro_sums() -> None:
    t = HeadingTotals().add(HeadingMatchV2(2, 3, 4, 1, 1)).add(HeadingMatchV2(1, 1, 1, 0, 0))
    assert (t.recall, t.precision, t.relative_level_agreement) == (3 / 5, 3 / 4, 1.0)


def test_blended_cost_matches_the_hand_computed_example() -> None:
    assert blended_cost_per_page(COUNTS, Prices(c_textin=0.14)) == pytest.approx(0.07)
    p = Prices(c_textin=0.14, c_local=0.01, l1_enabled=True, l1_pass=0.6)
    assert blended_cost_per_page(COUNTS, p) == pytest.approx(0.0404)
    assert cost_breakdown(COUNTS, Prices(c_textin=0.14))["table"] == pytest.approx(20 * 0.14 / 100)
    assert C_TEXTIN_MEASURED == pytest.approx(0.13556, abs=1e-5)


def test_cost_agrees_with_the_tiered_routing_plan_formula() -> None:
    scan, stmt, p0 = routing_cost_inputs(COUNTS)
    c = 0.14
    # 分级路由计划 T7 blended_cost_per_page：L0 开（c_l0 = 0）、L1 / L2 关
    routing = (1 - scan) * stmt * c + (1 - scan) * (1 - stmt) * (1 - p0) * c + scan * c
    assert routing == pytest.approx(blended_cost_per_page(COUNTS, Prices(c_textin=c)))
    assert (scan, stmt, p0) == pytest.approx((0.05, 5 / 95, 50 / 90))


def _passing(**over: object) -> L0Metrics:
    base: dict[str, object] = dict(
        closure_failed=0, audit_inconsistent=0, num_recall=0.995, unexplained_missing=0,
        multicol=MulticolRates(40, 1, 200, 10), heading=HeadingTotals(70, 80, 100, 50, 55),
        research_heading_available=120, research_heading_judged=100, research_heading_precision=0.9,
        leakage_rate=0.02, spot_available=60, spot_pages=30, spot_order_failures=0,
        coverage_announcement=0.4, coverage_research=0.2, multicol_share_announcement=0.05, seconds_p95=0.4,
        figure_pages_announcement=12, figure_page_recall=0.995, figure_table_number_loss=0.0,
        figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(pages=80, charts_total=40, charts_covered=36, boxes=45, boxes_chart=40,
                                        boxes_table_or_body=0),
    )
    base.update(over)
    return L0Metrics(**base)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_all_gates_pass_on_good_metrics_and_any_single_failure_is_no_go() -> None:
    assert is_go(evaluate_gates(_passing(), final=True))
    for over in [dict(closure_failed=1), dict(audit_inconsistent=1), dict(num_recall=0.98), dict(unexplained_missing=1),
                 dict(multicol=MulticolRates(40, 3, 200, 10)), dict(multicol=MulticolRates(20, 0, 200, 10)),
                 dict(multicol=None), dict(heading=HeadingTotals(50, 80, 100, 50, 55)),
                 dict(research_heading_precision=0.8), dict(research_heading_judged=40), dict(leakage_rate=0.06),
                 dict(spot_order_failures=1), dict(spot_pages=10), dict(coverage_research=0.1),
                 dict(multicol_share_announcement=0.2), dict(seconds_p95=2.0),
                 dict(figure_page_recall=0.98), dict(figure_table_number_loss=0.02),
                 dict(figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(80, 40, 30, 45, 40, 0)),  # 召回 0.75
                 dict(figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(80, 40, 36, 45, 40, 1)),  # 框住了表格 / 正文
                 dict(figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(80, 40, 36, 45, 30, 0)),  # 精确率 0.667
                 dict(figure_labels=None)]:
        assert not is_go(evaluate_gates(_passing(**over), final=True)), over


def test_human_gates_are_not_evaluated_on_the_tuning_set_and_g7_is_na_when_few_headings() -> None:
    tune = evaluate_gates(_passing(research_heading_judged=0, research_heading_precision=None,
                                   spot_pages=0, spot_order_failures=None, figure_labels=None), final=False)
    assert is_go(tune)
    na = evaluate_gates(_passing(research_heading_available=12, research_heading_judged=0,
                                 research_heading_precision=None), final=True)
    assert is_go(na)


def test_figure_gates_are_not_applicable_below_their_minimum_counts() -> None:
    few = _passing(figure_pages_announcement=5, figure_page_recall=None,
                   figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(80, 12, 6, 10, 5, 0))  # 图表 < 30、框 < 20
    assert is_go(evaluate_gates(few, final=True))
    bad = _passing(figure_labels=FigureLabelTotals(80, 12, 6, 10, 5, 1))  # G14b 没有"不适用"
    assert not is_go(evaluate_gates(bad, final=True))


def test_figure_labels_are_summed_and_blank_rows_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    p = tmp_path / "f.tsv"
    p.write_text(
        "set\tgroup\tkey\tpage\tboxes\tcharts_total\tcharts_covered\tbox_labels\tnote\n"
        "holdout\tresearch\tAP1\t2\t3\t2\t2\tchart,partial,other\t\n"
        "holdout\tdocs\t556\t1\t1\t0\t0\ttable\t\n"
        "holdout\tresearch\tAP2\t1\t0\t1\t0\t\tunsure\n"
        "tune\tresearch\tAP3\t1\t0\t\t\t\t\n", encoding="utf-8")
    t = read_figure_labels(p, "holdout")
    assert t == FigureLabelTotals(pages=2, charts_total=2, charts_covered=2, boxes=4, boxes_chart=2, boxes_table_or_body=1)
    assert (t.recall, t.precision) == (1.0, 0.5)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="未标注"):
        read_figure_labels(p, "tune")
    bad = tmp_path / "b.tsv"
    bad.write_text("set\tgroup\tkey\tpage\tboxes\tcharts_total\tcharts_covered\tbox_labels\tnote\n"
                   "holdout\tresearch\tAP1\t2\t2\t1\t1\tchart\t\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="框数"):
        read_figure_labels(bad, "holdout")


def test_the_holdout_can_be_claimed_only_once(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    lock = tmp_path / "holdout-run.lock"
    claim_holdout(lock, git_sha="abc", today=date(2026, 10, 20))
    assert lock.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "2026-10-20 abc\n"
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="只跑一次"):
        claim_holdout(lock, git_sha="def", today=date(2026, 10, 21))


def test_label_readers_reject_blank_labels_and_count_judgments(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    labels = tmp_path / "l.tsv"
    labels.write_text("set\tgroup\tkey\tpage\tlabel\tnote\nholdout\tresearch\tAP1\t1\tmulti\t\ntune\tdocs\t556\t1\t\t\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert read_multicol_labels(labels, "holdout") == {("research", "AP1", 1): "multi"}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="未标注"):
        read_multicol_labels(labels, "tune")
    j = tmp_path / "j.tsv"
    j.write_text("key\tpage\tline\tlevel\theading\tmd_path\tjudgment\tnote\nA\t1\t3\t2\t一、x\tp\tyes\t\nA\t1\t9\t3\ty\tp\tno\t\nA\t2\t1\t2\tz\tp\tunsure\t\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert read_judgments(j) == (1, 1)
    s = tmp_path / "s.tsv"
    s.write_text("group\tkey\tpage\tmd_path\torder_ok\tpara_ok\tnote\ndocs\t556\t1\tp\tyes\tno\t\nresearch\tAP1\t2\tp\tno\tyes\t\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert read_spotcheck(s) == (2, 1)


def test_sampling_is_deterministic_and_capped() -> None:
    items = list(range(50))
    assert sample_items(items, 10, 20261013) == sample_items(items, 10, 20261013)
    assert sorted(sample_items(items, 100, 1)) == items
```
